PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object b, object d craves object c, harmony, planet object b, planet object c, province object a and province object d.
My goal is to have that object c craves object a.

My plan is as follows:

[PLAN]
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

FINAL-PLAN

<domain>
(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types entity)
  (:predicates
    (province ?o - entity)
    (planet ?o - entity)
    (pain ?o - entity)
    (craves ?o1 ?o2 - entity)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?o - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and
      (pain ?o)
      (not (province ?o))
      (not (planet ?o))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?o - entity)
    :precondition (pain ?o)
    :effect (and
      (province ?o)
      (planet ?o)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?o))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?o - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?o))
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?o)
      (craves ?o ?other)
      (not (province ?other))
      (not (pain ?o))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?o - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (craves ?o ?other) (province ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and
      (pain ?o)
      (province ?other)
      (not (craves ?o ?other))
      (not (province ?o))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)
</domain>

<problem>
(define (problem joat_problem)
  (:domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:objects
    a b c d - entity
  )
  (:init
    (harmony)
    (craves a b)
    (craves d c)
    (planet b)
    (planet c)
    (province a)
    (province d)
  )
  (:goal
    (and
      (craves c a)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

(define (domain jack_of_all_trades)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types entity)
  (:predicates
    (province ?o - entity)
    (planet ?o - entity)
    (pain ?o - entity)
    (craves ?o1 ?o2 - entity)
    (harmony)
  )

  (:action attack
    :parameters (?o - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?o) (planet ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and
      (pain ?o)
      (not (province ?o))
      (not (planet ?o))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )

  (:action succumb
    :parameters (?o - entity)
    :precondition (pain ?o)
    :effect (and
      (province ?o)
      (planet ?o)
      (harmony)
      (not (pain ?o))
    )
  )

  (:action overcome
    :parameters (?o - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (province ?other) (pain ?o))
    :effect (and
      (harmony)
      (province ?o)
      (craves ?o ?other)
      (not (province ?other))
      (not (pain ?o))
    )
  )

  (:action feast
    :parameters (?o - entity ?other - entity)
    :precondition (and (craves ?o ?other) (province ?o) (harmony))
    :effect (and
      (pain ?o)
      (province ?other)
      (not (craves ?o ?other))
      (not (province ?o))
      (not (harmony))
    )
  )
)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_0
------------------------------
Based on the PDDL plan and specification provided, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Feast object d from object c**
*   **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Action:** Feast
*   **Details:** Begin by feasting object **d** from object **c**. This is possible because object **d** initially craves object **c**, object **d** is a province, and harmony exists. As a result of this action, object **d** will enter a state of pain, object **c** will become a province, and harmony will be lost.

**Step 2: Succumb object d**
*   **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Action:** Succumb
*   **Details:** Perform the succumb action on object **d**. Since object **d** is in a state of pain from the previous step, it can succumb. This action restores harmony and transforms object **d** into a province and a planet, while ending its state of pain.

**Step 3: Attack object c**
*   **Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Action:** Attack
*   **Details:** Attack object **c**. This action is now valid because object **c** became a province in Step 1, it is a planet (initial condition), and harmony was restored in Step 2. Performing this action puts object **c** into a state of pain and removes the province, planet, and harmony statuses.

**Step 4: Overcome object c from object a**
*   **Time:** 00:03 - 00:04
*   **Action:** Overcome
*   **Details:** Overcome object **c** from object **a**. This final action requires that object **a** is a province (initial condition) and object **c** is in a state of pain (from Step 3). Upon completion, harmony is restored, object **c** becomes a province, and the goal is achieved: **object c now craves object a.**

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the PDDL domain, problem, and plan provided, here is the clear sequence of human-readable actions required to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Feast on object c from object b**
(00:00 - 00:01)
Perform the feast action where object b is the source and object c is the target. This requires that object b currently craves object c and harmony is maintained. As a result, object b will no longer crave object c, harmony is lost, and object b enters a state of pain while object c becomes a province.

**Step 2: Succumb object b**
(00:01 - 00:02)
Perform the succumb action on object b. This alleviates the pain for object b, restoring harmony and establishing object b as both a province and a planet.

**Step 3: Feast on object d from object c**
(00:02 - 00:03)
Perform the feast action where object c is the source and object d is the target. Since object c is now a province and craves object d, this action transitions object c into a state of pain and makes object d a province, while breaking the initial craving and harmony.

**Step 4: Succumb object c**
(00:03 - 00:04)
Perform the succumb action on object c. This removes the pain from object c, returning the environment to a state of harmony and designating object c as a province and a planet.

**Step 5: Feast on object a from object d**
(00:04 - 00:05)
Perform the feast action where object d is the source and object a is the target. Object d (which craves a) transitions to a state of pain, and object a becomes a province. Harmony is temporarily lost.

**Step 6: Succumb object d**
(00:05 - 00:06)
Perform the succumb action on object d. This ends the pain for object d, restores harmony, and sets object d as a province and a planet.

**Step 7: Attack object a**
(00:06 - 00:07)
Perform the attack action on object a. Since object a is a planet and a province and harmony exists, attacking it results in object a entering a state of pain. Harmony, its province status, and its planet status are removed.

**Step 8: Overcome object a from object c**
(00:07 - 00:08)
Perform the overcome action to link object a to object c. This uses the pain of object a and the province status of object c to restore harmony and create a new relationship where **object a craves object c**.

**Step 9: Attack object d**
(00:08 - 00:09)
Perform the attack action on object d. This transitions object d (which was a province and planet under harmony) into a state of pain, removing its province/planet status and the state of harmony.

**Step 10: Overcome object d from object a**
(00:09 - 00:10)
Perform the overcome action to link object d to object a. This uses the pain of object d and the province status of object a to restore harmony and establish the final requirement: **object d craves object a**.

**Final Goal Reached:** Object a craves object c, and object d craves object a.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here are the step-by-step instructions to achieve the goal:

**Initial State:** Object A craves Object C, Object C craves Object B, Object D craves Object A. There is harmony, Object B is a planet, and Object D is a province.
**Goal:** Object A craves Object D and Object D craves Object B.

### Action Sequence

1.  **00:00 - Feast on Object D from Object A:**
    Since Object D craves Object A, Object D is a province, and there is harmony, feast on Object D. This causes Object D to feel pain and moves the province status to Object A, but breaks the harmony.

2.  **00:01 - Object D Succumbs:**
    Because Object D is in pain, it succumbs. This restores harmony and restores Object D’s status as a province and a planet, ending its pain.

3.  **00:02 - Feast on Object A from Object C:**
    Since Object A craves Object C, Object A is now a province, and harmony is restored, feast on Object A. Object A now feels pain, Object C becomes a province, and harmony is lost.

4.  **00:03 - Overcome Object A from Object D:**
    Using the fact that Object D is a province and Object A is in pain, perform an overcome action. This restores harmony, makes Object A a province, and establishes that **Object A craves Object D**.

5.  **00:04 - Feast on Object C from Object B:**
    Since Object C craves Object B, Object C is a province, and harmony exists, feast on Object C. Object C feels pain, Object B becomes a province, and harmony is lost.

6.  **00:05 - Object C Succumbs:**
    Object C, being in pain, succumbs. This restores harmony and sets Object C as a province and a planet.

7.  **00:06 - Feast on Object A from Object D:**
    Since Object A now craves Object D (from step 4) and is a province, feast on Object A. Object A feels pain, Object D becomes a province, and harmony is lost.

8.  **00:07 - Object A Succumbs:**
    Object A, being in pain, succumbs. Harmony is restored, and Object A becomes a province and a planet.

9.  **00:08 - Attack Object D:**
    Since Object D is a province and a planet and there is harmony, launch an attack on Object D. This results in Object D feeling pain and the loss of its province/planet status and harmony.

10. **00:09 - Overcome Object D from Object B:**
    With Object B acting as a province and Object D in pain, overcome Object D. This restores harmony, makes Object D a province, and establishes that **Object D craves Object B**.

11. **00:10 - Attack Object A:**
    With Object A as a province and a planet and harmony present, attack Object A. Object A enters a state of pain, and harmony is lost.

12. **00:11 - Overcome Object A from Object D:**
    Finally, using Object D as a province to help Object A overcome its pain, Object A is restored as a province, harmony returns, and it is reinforced that **Object A craves Object D**.

**Final Status:** The goal is met. Object A craves Object D, and Object D craves Object B.

Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here is the sequence of human-readable actions to achieve the goal:

**Action Sequence:**

1.  **00:00 - 00:01: Attack object d.**
    *   **Preconditions:** Object **d** must be a province and a planet, and the state must be in harmony.
    *   **Effect:** Object **d** will now be in a state of pain. It is no longer a province or a planet, and harmony is disrupted.

2.  **00:01 - 00:02: Overcome object d from object c.**
    *   **Preconditions:** Object **c** must be a province, and object **d** must be in a state of pain.
    *   **Effect:** Object **d** now craves object **c**. Harmony is restored, and object **d** becomes a province while object **c** is no longer a province.

**Final Goal Status:**
*   Object **b** craves object **a** (maintained from the initial state).
*   Object **d** craves object **c** (achieved in step 2).

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL domain, problem, and plan, here are the sequential human-readable instructions to achieve the goal:

**Goal:** Establish that object **a** craves object **b** and object **d** craves object **c**.

**Execution Plan:**

1.  **[00:01] Feast on object d from object a:** 
    Beginning with the initial state where harmony exists, object **d** is a province and craves object **a**, perform the feast action. This results in object **d** being in pain and object **a** becoming a province.

2.  **[00:02] Succumb object d:** 
    With object **d** currently in pain, allow it to succumb. This restores harmony and establishes object **d** as both a province and a planet.

3.  **[00:03] Feast on object a from object c:** 
    Since harmony has been restored and object **a** is now a province (from step 1), and initially craves object **c**, perform the feast action. This results in object **a** being in pain and object **c** becoming a province.

4.  **[00:04] Succumb object a:** 
    With object **a** currently in pain, allow it to succumb. This restores harmony and establishes object **a** as both a province and a planet.

5.  **[00:05] Feast on object c from object b:** 
    With harmony restored and object **c** now a province (from step 3), and initially craving object **b**, perform the feast action. This results in object **c** being in pain and object **b** becoming a province.

6.  **[00:06] Succumb object c:** 
    With object **c** currently in pain, allow it to succumb. This restores harmony and establishes object **c** as both a province and a planet.

7.  **[00:07] Attack object a:** 
    With object **a** set as a province and a planet (from step 4) and harmony existing, perform an attack on object **a**. This removes its status as a province/planet and leaves it in pain, disrupting harmony.

8.  **[00:08] Overcome object a from object b:** 
    Using the fact that object **b** is a province (from step 5) and object **a** is in pain, perform the overcome action. This restores harmony, establishes object **a** as a province, and successfully makes **object a crave object b**.

9.  **[00:09] Attack object d:** 
    With object **d** set as a province and a planet (from step 2) and harmony restored, perform an attack on object **d**. This removes its status as a province/planet and leaves it in pain, disrupting harmony.

10. **[00:10] Overcome object d from object c:** 
    Using the fact that object **c** is a province (from step 6) and object **d** is in pain, perform the overcome action. This restores harmony, establishes object **d** as a province, and successfully makes **object d crave object c**.

**Final Status:** The goal is met. Object **a** now craves object **b**, and object **d** now craves object **c**.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having object **a** crave object **c**, follow these instructions step-by-step:

**Step 1: Feast on object a from object d**
*   **Time:** 0:00 - 0:01
*   **Action:** Feast object **a** from object **d**.
*   **Effect:** This will result in object **a** feeling pain and object **d** being in the province, while the initial craving between **a** and **d** and the state of harmony are removed.

**Step 2: Succumb object a**
*   **Time:** 0:01 - 0:02
*   **Action:** Succumb object **a**.
*   **Effect:** This restores object **a** to the province and the planet, relieves its pain, and returns the environment to a state of harmony.

**Step 3: Feast on object d from object b**
*   **Time:** 0:02 - 0:03
*   **Action:** Feast object **d** from object **b**.
*   **Effect:** Object **d** will now feel pain, and object **b** will be in the province. The craving between **d** and **b** is satisfied, and harmony is temporarily lost.

**Step 4: Succumb object d**
*   **Time:** 0:03 - 0:04
*   **Action:** Succumb object **d**.
*   **Effect:** Object **d** is restored to the province and planet, its pain is relieved, and harmony is established again.

**Step 5: Feast on object b from object c**
*   **Time:** 0:04 - 0:05
*   **Action:** Feast object **b** from object **c**.
*   **Effect:** Object **b** will feel pain, and object **c** will move into the province. The craving between **b** and **c** is satisfied, and harmony is lost.

**Step 6: Succumb object b**
*   **Time:** 0:05 - 0:06
*   **Action:** Succumb object **b**.
*   **Effect:** Object **b** is restored to the province and planet, its pain is relieved, and harmony is restored once more.

**Step 7: Attack object a**
*   **Time:** 0:06 - 0:07
*   **Action:** Attack object **a**.
*   **Precondition:** Requires object **a** to be in the province and on the planet while harmony exists.
*   **Effect:** Object **a** will now feel pain. It is no longer in the province or on the planet, and harmony is broken.

**Step 8: Overcome object a from object c**
*   **Time:** 0:07 - 0:08
*   **Action:** Overcome object **a** from object **c**.
*   **Precondition:** Requires object **a** to be in pain and object **c** to be in the province.
*   **Effect:** Object **a** is restored to the province and its pain is relieved. Most importantly, **object a now craves object c**, satisfying the final goal. Harmony is also restored.
================================================================================

I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.

    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

Based on the provided plan and specification, here is the sequence of actions required to achieve the goal:

**Sequence of Actions:**

*   **Step 1: Feast object b from object c**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:00
    *   **Action:** Feast on object `b`, which currently craves object `c`. This action results in object `b` being in a state of "pain" and makes object `c` a "province," while harmony is temporarily lost.

*   **Step 2: Succumb object b**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:01
    *   **Action:** Resolve the pain state for object `b`. This restores harmony and establishes object `b` as both a "province" and a "planet."

*   **Step 3: Attack object a**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:02
    *   **Action:** Perform an attack on object `a`. Since object `a` is a "province" and a "planet," and harmony exists, this transitions object `a` into a state of "pain" and consumes the harmony.

*   **Step 4: Overcome object a from object b**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:03
    *   **Action:** Use object `a` to overcome object `b`. This restores harmony and results in object `a` craving object `b`.

*   **Step 5: Attack object d**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:04
    *   **Action:** Perform an attack on object `d`. This transitions object `d` (a province and planet) into a state of "pain," consuming the harmony.

*   **Step 6: Overcome object d from object c**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:05
    *   **Action:** Use object `d` to overcome object `c`. This restores harmony and results in object `d` craving object `c`.

*   **Step 7: Feast object a from object b**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:06
    *   **Action:** Feast on object `a` from object `b`. Object `a` enters a state of "pain," object `b` becomes a province, and harmony is lost.

*   **Step 8: Succumb object a**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:07
    *   **Action:** Resolve the pain state for object `a`, restoring harmony and making object `a` a province and a planet.

*   **Step 9: Attack object b**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:08
    *   **Action:** Perform an attack on object `b`. Object `b` enters a state of "pain," and harmony is lost.

*   **Step 10: Overcome object b from object d**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:09
    *   **Action:** Use object `b` to overcome object `d`. Harmony is restored, and object `b` now craves object `d`.

*   **Step 11: Attack object a**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:10
    *   **Action:** Perform an attack on object `a`. Object `a` enters a state of "pain," and harmony is lost.

*   **Step 12: Overcome object a from object b**
    *   **Timestamp:** 00:11
    *   **Action:** Use object `a` to overcome object `b`. This final action restores harmony and establishes that object `a` craves object `b`.

**Final Goal State:**
- Object `a` craves object `b`.
- Object `b` craves object `d`.
- Object `d` craves object `c`.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: Feast on object c from object d**
*   **Time:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Action:** Lift object c off from its position on top of object d. This releases object d while you hold object c, breaking the current harmony.

**Step 2: Succumb object c**
*   **Time:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Action:** Place object c down on the table (as a planet object). This makes object c clear (a province) and restores harmony to the environment.

**Step 3: Feast on object d from object a**
*   **Time:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Action:** Lift object d off from its position on top of object a. This releases object a while you hold object d, breaking harmony.

**Step 4: Overcome object d from object b**
*   **Time:** 00:03 - 00:04
*   **Action:** Stack object d onto the clear object b. Object d is now on top of object b (d craves b), object d is now clear, and harmony is restored.

**Step 5: Attack object a**
*   **Time:** 00:04 - 00:05
*   **Action:** Pick up object a from its position on the table. You are now holding object a, and harmony is broken.

**Step 6: Overcome object a from object c**
*   **Time:** 00:05 - 00:06
*   **Action:** Stack object a onto the clear object c. Object a is now on top of object c (a craves c), object a is now clear, and harmony is restored.

**Step 7: Feast on object d from object b**
*   **Time:** 00:06 - 00:07
*   **Action:** Lift object d off from its position on top of object b. This makes object b clear again while you hold object d, breaking harmony.

**Step 8: Succumb object d**
*   **Time:** 00:07 - 00:08
*   **Action:** Place object d down on the table. Object d is now clear on the table and harmony is restored.

**Step 9: Attack object b**
*   **Time:** 00:08 - 00:09
*   **Action:** Pick up object b from the table. You are now holding object b and harmony is broken.

**Step 10: Overcome object b from object a**
*   **Time:** 00:09 - 00:10
*   **Action:** Stack object b onto the clear object a. Object b is now on top of object a (b craves a), object b is now clear, and harmony is restored.

**Step 11: Attack object d**
*   **Time:** 00:10 - 00:11
*   **Action:** Pick up object d from its position on the table. You are now holding object d and harmony is broken.

**Step 12: Overcome object d from object b**
*   **Time:** 00:11 - 00:12
*   **Action:** Stack object d onto the clear object b. Object d is now on top of object b (d craves b), object d is clear, and harmony is restored.

**Final State Achieved:**
*   Object a is on object c.
*   Object b is on object a.
*   Object d is on object b.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goals of having object **a** crave object **d** and object **b** crave object **c**, the following sequence of actions must be performed. Each step occurs in a one-minute interval.

**Plan Execution:**

*   **Step 1 (00:00 - 00:01): Feast on object c from object b.**
    Initially, object **c** craves object **b**, and there is harmony. By feasting on **c**, the craving for **b** is removed, and object **b** is now established in a province, though harmony is temporarily lost and object **c** enters a state of pain.

*   **Step 2 (00:01 - 00:02): Succumb object c.**
    Object **c** yields to its state of pain. This action restores harmony and establishes object **c** as both a province and a planet.

*   **Step 3 (00:02 - 00:03): Feast on object d from object a.**
    Since object **d** craves object **a** and there is harmony, object **d** feasts. This ends the craving for **a** and places object **a** in a province, while harmony is lost again and object **d** enters a state of pain.

*   **Step 4 (00:03 - 00:04): Succumb object d.**
    Object **d** yields to its state of pain. This restores harmony and establishes object **d** as both a province and a planet.

*   **Step 5 (00:04 - 00:05): Attack object a.**
    With object **a** now in a province (from Step 3) and existing as a planet (from the start), and with harmony restored (from Step 4), an attack is launched on object **a**. This puts object **a** in a state of pain and disrupts harmony.

*   **Step 6 (00:05 - 00:06): Overcome object a from object d.**
    Using the province of object **d**, object **a** overcomes its state of pain. This results in object **a** now craving object **d** (Goal 1 achieved), restores harmony, and establishes **a** in a province.

*   **Step 7 (00:06 - 00:07): Attack object b.**
    With object **b** now in a province (from Step 1) and existing as a planet (from the start), and with harmony restored (from Step 6), an attack is launched on object **b**. This puts object **b** in a state of pain and disrupts harmony.

*   **Step 8 (00:07 - 00:08): Overcome object b from object c.**
    Using the province of object **c**, object **b** overcomes its state of pain. This results in object **b** now craving object **c** (Goal 2 achieved), restores harmony, and establishes **b** in a province.

**Final Status:**
The sequence is complete. Object **a** craves object **d**, and object **b** craves object **c**, fulfilling all requirements.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
I am playing with a set of objects. Here are the actions I can do

   Attack object
   Feast object from another object
   Succumb object
   Overcome object from another object

I have the following restrictions on my actions:
    To perform Attack action, the following facts need to be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be true: Pain object.
    Once Attack action is performed the following facts will be false: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.
    To perform Succumb action, the following facts need to be true: Pain object.
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be true: Province object, Planet object, Harmony.    
    Once Succumb action is performed the following facts will be false: Pain object.
    To perform Overcome action, the following needs to be true: Province other object, Pain object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be true: Harmony, Province object, Object Craves other object.
    Once Overcome action is performed the following will be false: Province other object, Pain object.
    To perform Feast action, the following needs to be true: Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be true: Pain object, Province other object.
    Once Feast action is performed the following will be false:, Object Craves other object, Province object, Harmony.

[STATEMENT]
As initial conditions I have that, object a craves object d, object b craves object c, harmony, planet object c, planet object d, province object a and province object b.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and specification, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Goal:** Ensure object **b** craves object **d**, and object **d** craves object **a**.

**Action Sequence:**

1.  **[00:00 - 00:01] Feast object d from object a.**
    *   *Initial state requirements:* Object **a** craves object **d**, and object **a** is in a province while harmony exists.
    *   *Result:* Object **a** is now in pain, object **d** is moved to a province, and object **a** no longer craves object **d**. Harmony is temporarily lost.

2.  **[00:01 - 00:02] Succumb object a.**
    *   *Requirement:* Object **a** must be in pain.
    *   *Result:* Pain for object **a** ends; object **a** becomes a province and a planet, and harmony is restored.

3.  **[00:02 - 00:03] Attack object d.**
    *   *Requirement:* Object **d** must be a province and a planet, and harmony must exist. (Object **d** became a province in step 1 and was a planet initially).
    *   *Result:* Object **d** is now in pain; it is no longer a province or planet, and harmony is lost.

4.  **[00:03 - 00:04] Overcome object d from object a.**
    *   *Requirement:* Object **a** must be a province and object **d** must be in pain.
    *   *Result:* Object **d** now craves object **a**. Harmony is restored, and object **d** becomes a province.

5.  **[00:04 - 00:05] Feast object c from object b.**
    *   *Requirement:* Object **b** craves object **c**, object **b** is a province, and harmony exists.
    *   *Result:* Object **b** is now in pain, object **c** is moved to a province, and object **b** no longer craves object **c**. Harmony is lost.

6.  **[00:05 - 00:06] Overcome object b from object d.**
    *   *Requirement:* Object **d** is a province and object **b** is in pain.
    *   *Result:* Object **b** now craves object **d**. Harmony is restored, and object **b** becomes a province.

**Final State:** The requirements are met—object **b** craves object **d**, and object **d** craves object **a**.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and specification, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: 00:00 - 00:05**
**Action:** Feast object **b** from object **d**.
*   **Context:** Initially, object **b** craves object **d**, and there is harmony. Object **b** is in a province. 
*   **Result:** This action causes object **b** to enter a state of pain and removes the craving for object **d**. Object **d** is now established in a province, and harmony is temporarily lost.

**Step 2: 00:05 - 00:10**
**Action:** Overcome object **b** from object **c**.
*   **Context:** Object **c** is currently in a province, and object **b** is in pain from the previous step.
*   **Result:** Object **b** overcomes its state through object **c**. This restores harmony, places object **b** back in a province, and establishes the goal condition that object **b** craves object **c**. Object **c** is no longer in a province.

**Step 3: 00:10 - 00:15**
**Action:** Attack object **d**.
*   **Context:** Object **d** was moved into a province in Step 1. It is also a planet object, and harmony was restored in Step 2.
*   **Result:** Attacking object **d** puts it into a state of pain. Consequently, object **d** is no longer in a province, its planet status is revoked, and harmony is lost again.

**Step 4: 00:15 - 00:20**
**Action:** Overcome object **d** from object **a**.
*   **Context:** Object **a** is in a province, and object **d** is in pain from the previous step.
*   **Result:** Object **d** overcomes its state through object **a**. This restores harmony and establishes the second goal condition: object **d** now craves object **a**. Object **a** is removed from its province.

**Final Goal State achieved:** Object **b** craves object **c**, and object **d** craves object **a**.
================================================================================

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and domain specification, here are the step-by-step instructions to achieve the goal where **object a craves object b**:

**Step 1: Feast object c from object b**
- **Timestamp:** 00:00
- **Action:** Perform a feast action using object c and object b.
- **Preconditions:** Object c must crave object b, object c must be in a province, and harmony must exist.
- **Result:** Object c will now feel pain and object b will be in a province. The previous craving, the province status of object c, and the state of harmony are removed.

**Step 2: Succumb object c**
- **Timestamp:** 00:01
- **Action:** Allow object c to succumb.
- **Preconditions:** Object c must be feeling pain (satisfied by Step 1).
- **Result:** Harmony is restored; object c is now both a province and a planet, and the pain for object c ceases.

**Step 3: Feast object d from object a**
- **Timestamp:** 00:02
- **Action:** Perform a feast action using object d and object a.
- **Preconditions:** Object d must crave object a, object d must be in a province, and harmony must exist (restored in Step 2).
- **Result:** Object d will now feel pain and object a will be in a province. The previous craving, the province status of object d, and the state of harmony are removed.

**Step 4: Succumb object d**
- **Timestamp:** 00:03
- **Action:** Allow object d to succumb.
- **Preconditions:** Object d must be feeling pain (satisfied by Step 3).
- **Result:** Harmony is restored; object d is now both a province and a planet, and the pain for object d ceases.

**Step 5: Attack object a**
- **Timestamp:** 00:04
- **Action:** Perform an attack on object a.
- **Preconditions:** Object a must be a province (satisfied by Step 3), a planet (initial condition), and harmony must exist (restored in Step 4).
- **Result:** Object a now feels pain. Its status as a province, its status as a planet, and the state of harmony are removed.

**Step 6: Overcome object a from object b**
- **Timestamp:** 00:05
- **Action:** Perform an overcome action involving object a and object b.
- **Preconditions:** Object a must be feeling pain (satisfied by Step 5) and object b must be in a province (satisfied by Step 1).
- **Result:** **Object a now craves object b.** Harmony is restored, object a becomes a province, the pain for object a ceases, and object b is no longer a province.

**Goal Reached:** Object a now craves object b.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and domain specifications, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**00:00 - 00:01: Action - Feast object a from object b**
Begin the process by performing a Feast action on object **a** using object **b**. This requires that object **a** craves object **b**, object **a** is in the province, and harmony exists. As a result, object **a** will experience pain, and object **b** will move into the province.

**00:01 - 00:02: Action - Succumb object a**
Perform the Succumb action on object **a**. Since object **a** is in pain, this action restores object **a** to the province and the planet while re-establishing harmony and ending its pain.

**00:02 - 00:03: Action - Feast object b from object c**
Perform a Feast action on object **b** using object **c**. This requires that object **b** craves object **c**, object **b** is in the province, and harmony exists. This results in object **b** experiencing pain while object **c** moves into the province.

**00:03 - 00:04: Action - Succumb object b**
Perform the Succumb action on object **b**. This alleviates the pain for object **b**, returning it to the province and planet and restoring harmony.

**00:04 - 00:05: Action - Feast object c from object d**
Perform a Feast action on object **c** using object **d**. This is possible because object **c** craves object **d** and is in the province with harmony present. This action causes object **c** to experience pain and moves object **d** into the province.

**00:05 - 00:06: Action - Succumb object c**
Perform the Succumb action on object **c**. This ends the pain for object **c**, placing it back in the province and planet while restoring harmony.

**00:06 - 00:07: Action - Attack object d**
Perform an Attack action on object **d**. Since object **d** is in the province and on a planet and harmony exists, the attack causes object **d** to experience pain and removes it from the province and planet, disrupting harmony.

**00:07 - 00:08: Action - Overcome object d from object a**
Perform the Overcome action to have object **d** overcome object **a**. Since object **a** is in the province and object **d** is in pain, harmony is restored, object **d** enters the province, and a craving is established where object **d** now craves object **a**.

**00:08 - 00:09: Action - Attack object c**
Perform an Attack action on object **c**. As object **c** is currently in the province and on a planet with harmony present, this results in object **c** experiencing pain and being removed from the province and planet.

**00:09 - 00:10: Action - Overcome object c from object d**
Perform the Overcome action to have object **c** overcome object **d**. Since object **d** is in the province and object **c** is in pain, harmony is restored, object **c** enters the province, and object **c** now craves object **d**.

**00:10 - 00:11: Action - Attack object b**
Perform an Attack action on object **b**. As object **b** is in the province and on a planet with harmony present, the attack causes object **b** to experience pain and removes it from the province and planet.

**00:11 - 00:12: Action - Overcome object b from object c**
Perform the final Overcome action to have object **b** overcome object **c**. Since object **c** is in the province and object **b** is in pain, harmony is restored, object **b** enters the province, and object **b** now craves object **c**.

**Final Goal State Reached:**
- Object **b** craves object **c**
- Object **c** craves object **d**
- Object **d** craves object **a**

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goals of having object **a** crave object **c** and object **b** crave object **a**, please follow these instructions:

**Step 1: Feast object c from object b**
- **Time:** 0:00 - 0:01
- **Description:** Initiate a feast on object **c** from object **b**. This action is possible because object **c** currently craves object **b**, object **c** is in a province, and there is harmony. As a result, object **c** will be in pain, object **b** will move to a province, and harmony will be lost.

**Step 2: Succumb object c**
- **Time:** 0:01 - 0:02
- **Description:** Allow object **c** to succumb. Since object **c** is in pain, this action restores object **c** to its status as a province and planet object, and restores harmony to the environment.

**Step 3: Attack object a**
- **Time:** 0:02 - 0:03
- **Description:** Perform an attack on object **a**. This is permitted because object **a** is currently a province and planet object and harmony exists. Performing this attack causes object **a** to enter a state of pain while removing its status as a province/planet and disrupting harmony.

**Step 4: Overcome object a from object c**
- **Time:** 0:03 - 0:04
- **Description:** Overcome object **a** using object **c**. Since object **c** is currently in a province and object **a** is in pain, this action results in object **a** craving object **c** (Goal 1). Harmony is restored, and object **a** is established in a province.

**Step 5: Feast object b from object d**
- **Time:** 0:04 - 0:05
- **Description:** Initiate a feast on object **b** from object **d**. Because object **b** craves object **d**, is in a province (from Step 1), and harmony exists, this can be performed. This results in object **b** being in pain and object **d** moving to a province, while harmony is once again lost.

**Step 6: Overcome object b from object a**
- **Time:** 0:05 - 0:06
- **Description:** Finally, overcome object **b** using object **a**. With object **a** now in a province and object **b** in pain, this action causes object **b** to crave object **a** (Goal 2). This also restores harmony and establishes object **b** in a province.

**Final Goal State:** Object **a** now craves object **c**, and object **b** now craves object **a**. All conditions are satisfied.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To achieve the goal of having object **c** crave object **b** and object **d** crave object **a**, the following sequence of actions should be performed:

**Step 1: Feast object d from object c**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:00 - 00:01
*   **Description:** Initiate the feast process for object **d** from object **c**. This action is possible because object **d** initially craves object **c**, **d** is a province, and harmony is present. As a result of this action, object **d** enters a state of pain, object **c** becomes a province, and the initial craving and harmony are lost.

**Step 2: Overcome object d from object a**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:01 - 00:02
*   **Description:** Use object **a** to allow object **d** to overcome its state. This is possible because object **a** is a province and object **d** is currently in pain. This action successfully results in object **d** craving object **a** (part one of the goal), restores harmony, and makes object **d** a province again, while object **a** is no longer a province.

**Step 3: Attack object c**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:02 - 00:03
*   **Description:** Perform an attack on object **c**. This is permissible because object **c** is now a province (from Step 1), it is a planet, and harmony has been restored. Following the attack, object **c** enters a state of pain, and its status as a province and planet is removed, along with the state of harmony.

**Step 4: Overcome object c from object b**
*   **Timestamp:** 00:03 - 00:04
*   **Description:** Use object **b** to allow object **c** to overcome its state of pain. This is possible because object **b** is a province and object **c** is in pain. Completion of this action results in object **c** craving object **b** (part two of the goal), restores harmony, and establishes object **c** as a province, while object **b** loses its province status.

**Final Goal State:**
*   Object **c** craves object **b**.
*   Object **d** craves object **a**.
*   The sequence is complete.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the plan provided, here is the sequence of actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: 00:00 - Feast object a from object d**
Initially, object **a** craves object **d**, a state of harmony exists, and object **a** is in a province. By performing this action, object **a** transitions into a state of pain, and the province status shifts to object **d**. Harmony is temporarily lost, and object **a** no longer craves object **d**.

**Step 2: 00:01 - Succumb object a**
Since object **a** is in pain, it must succumb. This action relieves the pain from object **a** and restores harmony. Additionally, object **a** is now recognized as both a planet and being in a province.

**Step 3: 00:02 - Feast object d from object c**
With object **d** now in a province and harmony restored, and knowing that object **d** craves object **c**, a feast is performed. Object **d** enters a state of pain, the province status shifts to object **c**, harmony is lost, and the craving between **d** and **c** is removed.

**Step 4: 00:03 - Succumb object d**
Object **d**, currently in pain, undergoes the succumb action. This restores harmony to the environment and removes the pain from object **d**. Object **d** is now established as a planet and as being in a province.

**Step 5: 00:04 - Attack object c**
Object **c** is currently in a province and is a planet, while harmony is present. Attacking object **c** causes it to enter a state of pain. Consequently, object **c** is no longer considered a planet or in a province, and harmony is broken.

**Step 6: 00:05 - Overcome object c from object d**
Using object **d** (which is in a province) to address object **c**'s pain, the overcome action is performed. This results in object **c** craving object **d** (fulfilling half of the goal). Harmony is restored, object **c** is now in a province, and the pain is removed from object **c**. Object **d** is no longer in a province.

**Step 7: 00:06 - Attack object b**
Object **b** is a planet in a province, and harmony exists. Attacking object **b** puts it into a state of pain. As a result, object **b** loses its planet and province status, and harmony is broken.

**Step 8: 00:07 - Overcome object b from object c**
Using object **c** (which is now in a province) to address object **b**'s pain, the final overcome action is performed. This results in object **b** craving object **c** (fulfilling the second half of the goal). Harmony is restored, object **b** is now in a province, the pain is removed from object **b**, and object **c** is no longer in a province.

**Goal Reached:** Object **b** now craves object **c**, and object **c** craves object **d**.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the PDDL domain, problem, and plan provided, here is the sequence of human-readable actions to achieve the goal:

**Step 1: 00:00 - Feast object d from object b**  
By performing the feast action, you unstack object d from object b. This action requires that object d was clear (province) and you had harmony. As a result, you are now holding object d (pain), and object b becomes clear (province).

**Step 2: 00:01 - Succumb object d**  
Place object d down onto the table. This restores harmony and sets object d as both a clear object (province) and an object on the table (planet).

**Step 3: 00:02 - Feast object b from object a**  
Unstack object b from object a. Since object b became clear in the first step, you can now feast on it. This results in you holding object b (pain) and object a becoming clear (province).

**Step 4: 00:03 - Succumb object b**  
Place object b down onto the table. This restores harmony and designates object b as a clear object on the table.

**Step 5: 00:04 - Feast object a from object c**  
Unstack object a from object c. Now that object a is clear, this action moves object a into your possession (pain) and makes object c clear (province).

**Step 6: 00:05 - Succumb object a**  
Place object a down onto the table. Harmony is restored, and object a is now a clear object on the table.

**Step 7: 00:06 - Attack object c**  
Pick up object c from the table. Object c was originally at the base (planet), and now that it is clear (province) and harmony exists, you can "attack" it to pick it up (pain).

**Step 8: 00:07 - Overcome object c from object d**  
Stack object c onto object d. This action requires that object d is clear and you are holding object c. Upon completion, object c now "craves" (is on top of) object d, and harmony is restored.

**Step 9: 00:08 - Attack object b**  
Pick up object b from the table. Since object b was placed on the table earlier and is clear, you "attack" it to take it into your possession (pain).

**Step 10: 00:09 - Overcome object b from object c**  
Stack object b onto object c. This action requires that object c is clear and you are holding object b. Upon completion, object b now "craves" (is on top of) object c.

**Goal Reached:** Object b now craves object c, and object c now craves object d.
